### skill: Listings · Directory · Auction — Architecture & Knowledge (BUILT v1.5.0)
**→ skills/listings-auction-directory-architecture.md**


> Consolidates three deep-research passes (best-in-class portals, cutting-edge proptech, IL directory data sources) + the infrastructure shipped in nadlan-config **v1.5.0**. Written provider-agnostic where possible for **reuse on Justice.co.il**. Owner brief 2026-06-01: free SEO cards for all IL projects+contractors+service-givers (claim-to-upgrade), Zillow-grade listings, online auctions, "levels above competitors", deeply wired, latest tech.

---

## 0. What is already BUILT and shipped (v1.5.0, on feature branch / PR #4)

Modular includes under `plugins/nadlan-config/inc/`, wired via `require_once` loop in the main plugin, healthcheck reports readiness. **Nothing live until merge→main + WP "Update".**

| Module | What it does |
|---|---|
| `catalog-meta.php` | REST meta for `nadlan_project` + `nadlan_professional`; shared claim/ownership/provenance meta on all 3 card CPTs. New cards default `claim_status=unclaimed`. |
| `claim.php` | `nadlan_claim` request CPT; public `POST /nadlan/v1/claim` (honeypot+rate-limit); admin "Approve & assign owner" meta box → creates/links a subscriber + sets `owner_user_id`, `claim_status=verified`; `map_meta_cap` lets a verified owner edit ONLY their own card + upload files. |
| `import.php` | data.gov.il CKAN importer. Contractors (res `4eb61bd6-18cf-4e7c-9f9c-e166dfa0a2d8`, ~14k) → `nadlan_professional`; urban-renewal (res `f65a0daf-f737-49c5-9424-d378d52104f5`, ~938) → `nadlan_project`. Idempotent upsert by `source_id`. Dashboard "Import next 500" buttons + `wp nadlan import contractors|urban` WP-CLI + `POST /nadlan/v1/import-enrich` for the Cowork/ChatGPT enrichment push. |
| `auction.php` | `nadlan_auction` CPT + custom `wp_nadlan_bids` table (dbDelta). Proxy/auto-bid, hidden reserve, soft-close anti-sniping, buyer's premium. `auctions/v1` REST: `/{id}/state`, `/{id}/bids` (GET history, POST place-bid). Per-auction `GET_LOCK` serializes bids. Hourly cron closes ended auctions. |
| `schema.php` | JSON-LD per card (GeneralContractor / ApartmentComplex / RealEstateListing / Event). **Thin-content noindex guard**: stub cards (data_quality≠enriched, <80 words, unclaimed) get `noindex,follow` via `wp_robots` + `wpseo_robots`. |
| `cards-render.php` | Appends facts table + media gallery + claim CTA (JS→claim REST) + provenance line to card single views. `[nadlan_card]` shortcode. Scoped CSS/JS. |

**Operate it:** WP Admin → Dashboard → "NadLan Directory Import" widget → click "Import next 500" repeatedly (or run WP-CLI) → cards appear under /professionals/ and /projects/ as `stub` (noindexed) → Cowork enriches each via `import-enrich` (original Hebrew prose) → card flips to `enriched` + indexable + IndexNow-pinged.

---

## 1. The strategic thesis (why this wins)

- **IL has no productized listing+auction+directory leader that's open and data-rich AND conversion-optimized.** Yad2 = inventory volume; Madlan = data depth; neither nails proactive alerts, seller funnels, or auctions. (See competitor read in §2.)
- **Free auto-cards = SEO land-grab.** ~14k contractors + ~938 renewal compounds + projects + service providers = thousands of branded/navigational pages we own before the entity does. Entity finds its #1-ranking card → claims → upgrades → marketing-platform revenue. The SEO does the selling (same insight as the contract-audit research).
- **Stats-rich original cards read as a knowledge source** → Google + AI answer-engines prefer them. Numbers aren't copyrightable; only phrasing is → generate original prose from structured fields (never paste source text).

---

## 2. Competitor read (verified research)

- **Zillow**: Zestimate AVM, "what's my home worth" seller funnel, mortgage calcs, 3D Home/SkyTour, saved search.
- **Redfin**: Redfin Estimate, **Hot Homes** (predicted-to-sell), days-on-market + favorite/tour counts as social proof, "Go Tour" CTA.
- **Realtor.com**: noise indicator, Flood Factor; surfaces price/schools/commute/crime/noise.
- **Rightmove**: sold-price history (Land Registry), full-screen floorplans, School Checker per listing.
- **Yad2 (IL)**: largest inventory, amenity checklist, neighborhood 0–5 user rating, map. *(JS-rendered; verify live.)*
- **Madlan (IL)**: data depth — median price, ₪/sqm, demographics, resident reviews, transaction history, planning/תב"ע, claims even sunlight; pulls Tax Authority + CBS. *(Verify sunlight/planning claims.)*

**To beat both:** Madlan-grade data + Redfin/Zillow conversion mechanics + Yad2-grade inventory, in proper Hebrew RTL.

---

## 3. Listings feature roadmap (the "Zillow" build — NOT yet built, prioritized)

Top must-builds (from research, ranked by SEO+conversion value):
1. **Nearby deal-history + AVM estimate** on every listing (govmap/nadlan deals → cached table → comps median). Madlan's moat.
2. **Neighborhood data panel** (median ₪/sqm, trend chart, demographics, resident reviews) — CBS + cached deals.
3. **Rich media**: photo + floorplan + video + 3D/360 (Kuula/Zillow 3D free-tier embeds), full-screen RTL gallery.
4. **Saved searches + email/WhatsApp/push alerts** (accounts + WP-Cron matcher).
5. **"How much is my apartment worth" seller lead funnel** (AVM → email + capture lead).
6. **One-tap WhatsApp + תיאום ביקור (schedule viewing)** flow.
7. **School + planning (תב"ע) overlays** (data.gov.il schools, govmap planning layers).
8. **Compare + favorites synced across devices**.
9. **Localized משכנתא/affordability calculator in ₪**.
10. **Programmatic SEO**: per-neighborhood landing pages from deal/CBS data + JSON-LD RealEstateListing.

IL-specific: full RTL, **גוש/חלקה/תת-חלקה** as first-class meta (join key to gov data), ועדה מקומית + תב"ע, מחיר למשתכן flag, IL listing types (דירת גן/פנטהאוז/דו-משפחתי/קוטג'/מגרש/מסחרי) + amenities (ממ"ד/מעלית/חניה/מרפסת/ארנונה/ועד בית).

**Cutting-edge "build now, high impact / low cost"** (from proptech scout): AI listing descriptions (LLM, pennies), JSON-LD (done), 3D tours (free embeds), AI virtual staging (disclose!), comps-based AVM lead magnet, similar-listings (SQL), e-sign on auction win (BoldSign/Dropbox Sign), NL search (LLM→filters), WhatsApp click-to-chat + chatbot, instant tour booking, image auto-tagging. **SKIP (hype):** blockchain tokenization / on-chain deeds (<0.1% market, no legal recognition, no liquidity).

---

## 4. Verified IL data sources (for importer + enrichment)

- **רשם הקבלנים** (the ~14k seed): dataset `data.gov.il/dataset/pinkashakablanim`, resource `4eb61bd6-18cf-4e7c-9f9c-e166dfa0a2d8`. CKAN: `data.gov.il/api/3/action/datastore_search?resource_id=...&limit=&offset=`. Key cols: `MISPAR_KABLAN` (canonical id), `SHEM_YESHUT` (name), `MISPAR_YESHUT` (company id), `SHEM_YISHUV` (city), `SHEM_REHOV`/`MISPAR_BAIT`, `MISPAR_TEL`, `EMAIL`, `KOD_ANAF`/`TEUR_ANAF` (branch), `SIVUG` (1–5 classification), `KABLAN_MUKAR`. One contractor = multiple rows (dedupe on MISPAR_KABLAN, aggregate branches). Branch codes: gov.il/he/departments/general/anfey_rishum_pinkas_hakablanim.
- **התחדשות עירונית** (תמ"א38/פינוי-בינוי, ~938): dataset `data.gov.il/dataset/urban_renewal`, resource `f65a0daf-f737-49c5-9424-d378d52104f5`. Cols: `MisparMitham`, `Yeshuv`+`SemelYeshuv`, `ShemMitcham`, `YachadKayam`/`YachadTosafti`/`YachadMutza`, `TaarichHachraza`, `MisparTochnit`, `KishurLatar`, `Maslul`, `ShnatMatanTokef`, `Bebitzua`, `Status`.
- **Deals/price** (nadlan.gov.il, Tax Authority): NO official API — UI only; ToS caution; State Comptroller 2025 flags data quality. Use for ₪/sqm enrichment, not primary seed. govmap layer `?lay=NADLAN`.
- **govmap** GIS/parcels/planning: `govmap.gov.il/sites/api_examples.html`. Community clients (nadlan-mcp etc.) report deal/parcel endpoints — paths reverse-engineered, **re-verify**. Build a caching ETL layer (cron → custom tables), never live per-pageview.
- **CBS/הלמ"ס**: building permits (statistical, not project-level), locality demographics.
- **Schools**: data.gov.il Ministry of Education dataset (search `package_search`, verify resource_id).
- **OSM Overpass** (`overpass-api.de/api/interpreter`): POI/transit for "what's nearby".

Build sequencing: Phase 1 contractors (clean CSV) → Phase 2 urban-renewal → Phase 3 new-build projects (scrape, messy) → Phase 4 service providers (per-profession registries: שמאים מועצת שמאי המקרקעין, אדריכלים פנקס המהנדסים, עו"ד לשכת עוה"ד, יועצי משכנתא = no registry → mark self-reported).

---

## 5. Original-content generation (anti-plagiarism)

Feed ONLY structured fields (numbers/dates/codes) to ChatGPT, instruct 100% original Hebrew Wikipedia-style prose, 120–220 words, no marketing, end with provenance line. Per-type prompt variants (project=timeline/price; contractor=branch table; service=credential+verification level). Run a near-duplicate (MinHash/shingling) check across generated cards to avoid templated cannibalization. Push result via `POST /nadlan/v1/import-enrich`. (Delegate the batch to Cowork — same pipeline as article batches.)

---

## 6. Claim / verification model (anti-hijack) — built skeleton + TODO

Built: claim request → admin approve → owner assigned → owner edits own card. **Verification METHOD is the hardening TODO** (see questions doc). Best-practice layered model to implement:
1. Match canonical id (MISPAR_KABLAN + company id / license no.) against registry row.
2. **OTP to the registry-listed phone/email** (`MISPAR_TEL`/`EMAIL`), NOT a typed contact — the key anti-hijack step (Google-postcard principle).
3. Domain/email match → instant verify.
4. Document upload fallback → human review.
5. Manual/video review for contested/high-value claims.
6. Hardening: never reveal OTP via support; re-verify on owner transfer; queue competing claims; rate-limit; immutable edit log; pre-claim card is read-only public data so a hijack can't alter facts.

---

## 7. Auction engine spec (BUILT — reference)

Timed English auctions (Auction.com/Hubzu model). Data: `nadlan_auction` CPT + `wp_nadlan_bids` table. Proxy/auto-bid: bidder submits `max_amount`; system shows min needed to lead = one increment above next-highest max. Hidden reserve → `reserve_met` flag. Soft-close: bid within `soft_close_window_sec` (default 120) extends end by `soft_close_extend_sec` (default 120). Buyer's premium % on win. Server-side validation + per-auction `GET_LOCK` (no double-winner). Hourly cron closes; on-page countdown should also re-check at zero. **TODO**: deposit holds + capture via Grow/Meshulam; e-sign on win (BoldSign/Dropbox Sign, ~48h window); true realtime (Pusher/Ably) vs current /state polling; system cron for second-level precision (WP-Cron is minute-ish).

---

## 8. Cannibalization discipline (HARD RULE)

- Entity cards = **navigational/branded** intent (the proper noun only). canonical = the card. Org/LocalBusiness/RealEstateListing schema.
- Generic keyword pages (e.g. "קבלן רשום בתל אביב", "תמ"א 38 מדריך") = hub/category pages that rank for the keyword and **link DOWN** to cards — never duplicated by cards.
- One URL = one intent. Cross-check every slug+focus-keyword against the **100-page inventory** (site-state.md cannibalization map).
- Thin/stub cards = `noindex,follow` until enriched (BUILT in schema.php). Facet/empty city combos should noindex too.
- Near-duplicate check across generated card bodies (MinHash).

---

## 8b. AVM / valuation methodology (BUILT v1.7.0 — `inc/avm-deals.php`)

Research-grounded (2025–26 best practice):
- **AVMs blend methods:** comparable-sales (comps), **hedonic regression** (value from features: sqm, rooms, age, location, sale timing), repeat-sales, and tax-assessment models. Modern AVMs are ML (gradient boosting: XGBoost/LightGBM/CatBoost; RF; sometimes LSTM/Transformer for time trends) and report a **confidence score / Forecast Standard Deviation (FSD)**. Leaders claim ~2% median error on-market, ~90–94% accuracy; 2026 trend = **explainable AI (SHAP)** for interpretable reports (needed in regulated/lending use).
- **What we built (v1.7.0):** a comparable-sales AVM as the pragmatic phase-1 — median ₪/sqm of nearby comps (same city, sqm ±25%, rooms ±1, last 36 months, 10% trimmed for robustness) × subject sqm; confidence = (1−coefficient-of-variation) scaled by sample size; ± band 5–25% from dispersion. Returns `insufficient_data` under 5 comps so it never shows a junk number. Reads ONLY from the cached `wp_nadlan_deals` table (never calls upstream per pageview).
- **Data ingest, two paths (decoupled from endpoint reverse-engineering):** (a) `nadlan_deals_remote` filter where Cowork's verified govmap/nadlan endpoint (mission M10) slots in for a cron ETL; (b) `POST /nadlan/v1/deals-ingest` so Cowork can push verified deal rows directly. Either fills the cache; the AVM works off it regardless.
- **Surfaces:** on-listing valuation block (estimate + range + comp count + confidence% + "not an appraisal" disclaimer), neighborhood 12-mo stats, and a `[nadlan_home_value]` **seller lead funnel** (estimate + captures phone → lead).
- **Roadmap upgrade:** replace median-comps with a trained gradient-boosting model + SHAP explanation once enough deal rows are cached; add repeat-sales index per neighborhood; price-trend charts.
- **BLANK (legal):** storing nadlan.gov.il price data needs ToS/legal sign-off (no official API). Govmap deal endpoints are reverse-engineered — verify (M10) before wiring the live ETL.

## 8c. AI features (BUILT v1.9.0 — `inc/ai-features.php`) + compliance discipline

**Hard rule, baked into the prompt AND a post-generation scanner:** no steering language by protected class. HUD (USA, 2024) and Israel's **חוק איסור הפליה במוצרים, בשירותים ובכניסה למקומות ציבוריים** both apply to AI-generated marketing copy — the platform is legally responsible for what it publishes. Penalties are real (US: per-violation can exceed $100k).

Banned phrases (non-exhaustive; reviewed list lives in code):
- Family/age: "מתאים למשפחות עם ילדים", "ל-zugot צעירים", "great for young professionals", "perfect for families".
- Religion/ethnicity: "קרוב לבית כנסת/מסגד/כנסייה", "שכונה דתית/חרדית/חילונית/ערבית/יהודית", "close to church/synagogue/mosque".
- Disability: "walking distance" (ableist) → "near".
- Exclusionary: "exclusive neighborhood".

What v1.9.0 ships:
- `nadlan_llm_request($system,$user,$opts)` delegates to the shared `nadlan_ai_chat()` provider adapter (default OpenAI, Anthropic fallback). Never fails open: returns `WP_Error` on missing key.
- **AI description generator** (admin meta box on `nadlan_property`): reads facts → prompts the LLM with the guardrails → runs `nadlan_compliance_scan()` → if hits, stores as DRAFT + flags for editor; if clean, editor still must click "Approve & write to content". Never auto-publishes.
- **Natural-language search** (`GET /nadlan/v1/nl-search?q=...` + `[nadlan_nl_search]`): LLM parses Hebrew → strict-JSON filter → reuses `nadlan_ss_meta_query()` → returns items + the parsed filter for transparency. **Deterministic regex fallback** for Hebrew patterns (`N חדרים`, `עד N מיליון`, `ב<עיר>`, `שכירות`/`מכירה`) keeps it working even if LLM is down. 1-hour transient cache per query.

Research grounding: Realmo Rey, planetRE+DeepSeek, Zillow's NL search (May 2026 launches); 2026 best-practice = LLM→structured filter→deterministic query, with cache + fallback.

**BLANK (owner):** pick LLM provider + add `NADLAN_LLM_API_KEY` to wp-config.php. Compliance list is conservative; review with counsel before relaxing. NL-search UX (autocomplete, history, voice) is roadmap.

## 8d. Programmatic SEO city hubs (BUILT v1.10.0 — `inc/city-hubs.php`) — 2026 discipline

**2026 reality (research):** Google now treats thin programmatic pages as **doorway/scaled-content abuse** with manual-action risk. The bar is much higher than 2022. Best practice:
- **≥25-30% UNIQUE data per URL** (not boilerplate). Real numbers, real entities, not "nice place to live".
- **Quality > volume.** 10 strong neighborhood hubs > 100 thin ones. Start with priority cities; expand from data.
- Zillow's model = hyper-local pages with home values + price trends + schools + walkability — context-rich, not template-stuffed.

What v1.10.0 ships:
- Rewrite endpoints `/city/<city>/{contractors|projects|properties}/` (no DB pages — clean, cache-friendly).
- **Card-count floor = 5** (configurable): under the floor → 404 (no thin page enters the index). Between 5 and the doubled floor → `noindex,follow`. Above → indexable.
- Per-hub unique data: count, AVG ₪/sqm + 12-mo deal count from the `wp_nadlan_deals` cache, plus top cards rendered inline.
- `CollectionPage` + `ItemList` JSON-LD, `meta description` derived from real counts, dedicated `/sitemap-nadlan-hubs.xml` (linked into Yoast sitemap index) that only includes hubs above the floor.
- Per-kind UP-link: contractor hubs → `/real-estate-lawyer/` pillar; project hubs → `/contract-audit/` product; property hubs → `[nadlan_save_search]` alert form. Cannibalization-safe (hub keyword = generic; cards = branded).

## 8e. Rich media (BUILT v1.10.0 — `inc/media.php`)

- **Kuula** is the recommended 3D-tour platform (Matterport dropped by Zillow Oct 2025 after CoStar acquired it; Kuula is the approved Zillow provider). We support: Kuula iframe (JS-style for iOS/perf, post-id auto-extracted), generic iframe (CloudPano/Panoee), video via WP oEmbed (YouTube/Vimeo), floorplan image or PDF (iframe).
- Tabbed UI on property singles. `VideoObject` JSON-LD when a video is set (rich-result eligible).
- Meta: `tour_url`, `video_url`, `floorplan_url` (REST exposed).

## 8f. Compare + Nearby POI + e-Sign (BUILT v1.11.0)

- **Compare listings** (`inc/compare.php`): Zillow/Redfin-grade side-by-side. localStorage tray (no auth), `/compare/` rewrite page, REST `/nadlan/v1/compare`. Cap 4 items; per-property ₪/sqm, amenities (elevator/parking/protected_room) rendered.
- **Nearby POI** (`inc/nearby-poi.php`): Realtor.com / Rightmove "what's nearby" parity via **OpenStreetMap Overpass API** — free, no key, 10k req/day/IP. Schools/kindergartens/transit/shops/health within 1km. **24h transient cache per coord-bucket** so we never burn rate. Fail-silent if Overpass is down.
- **e-Sign adapter** (`inc/esign.php`): pluggable, hooks `nadlan_auction_closed`. Default = email Hebrew offer letter to winner + admin; real providers (BoldSign/Dropbox Sign/DocuSign) plug in via `nadlan_esign_create_request` filter and return a hosted signing URL. **⚠ IL LAW (חוק חתימה אלקטרונית התשס"א-2001):** e-signatures are valid in IL — EXCEPT for property transactions, conveyances, and land-registry filings (and wills, notarized deeds, POAs, bank-customer agreements). So the adapter is scoped to the **offer/engagement letter**, NOT the שטר מכר. The disclaimer is baked into every generated document. **Counsel review required before go-live.**

## 8g. Map + Lead-drip (BUILT v1.12.0)

- **Leaflet archive map** (`inc/map.php`): Madlan/Yad2-style. Leaflet + leaflet.markercluster from CDN, OSM tiles (no key), RTL-aware. REST `/nadlan/v1/map?city=&listing_type=` returns lat/lng/title/price/url (≤500). `[nadlan_map]` shortcode + auto-prepends on `/properties/` archive. Fit-bounds when multiple.
- **Lead drip** (`inc/lead-drip.php`): 2026 best-practice — auto-respond ack on lead creation; **6-step Hebrew sequence** at days 0/2/5/14/30/60 (welcome, "5 דברים לבדוק לפני חוזה" → links to `/real-estate-lawyer/` + `/contract-audit/`, saved-search prompt, check-in, market-update, re-engage). **State machine**: active (0-14d) → mid (15d-6mo) → long (6-18mo) → archive (>540d). Every email has an opt-out token + REST that flips state to `optout` (silently stops sends). Filter `nadlan_drip_steps` lets owner customize the sequence. **BLANKS**: SMS/WhatsApp channel (Twilio/Vonage), engagement tracking pixel (IL Privacy Protection Law decision), branded HTML template, A-leads phone-escalation.

## 9. REUSE → Justice.co.il

The whole pattern is portable: free-card land-grab → claim → upgrade → marketing platform; CKAN/registry importer; original-content pipeline; thin-content noindex guard; auction engine; cannibalization discipline. For Justice.co.il swap entity types (lawyers/courts/legal-topics/rulings) and registries (לשכת עוה"ד, court databases). Keep modules provider-agnostic. **Standing instruction: keep recording all research + patterns into skills/ as the reusable asset.**

## 2. CONTENT CONSTRUCTION TEMPLATES (החלק הקריטי לצוות התוכן)

### 2.1 פירוק 5 דפים מובילים אצל המתחרים [crawled]

#### A. `nadlanmaster.co.il/מחשבון-מס-רכישה/` — 13.75% מהתעבורה של האתר
- **H1**: "מחשבון מס רכישה" (קצר, exact-match)
- **Above the fold**: שני H3 שמתפקדים כ-intro ("לפניכם מחשבון לחישוב…", "ניתן לחשב עבור דירה ראשונה/שנייה"). ה-Calculator עצמו מופיע מיד אחרי.
- **Block order**: H1 → קרדיט מערכת → הסבר 2 שורות → **מחשבון interactive (קלט מחיר + רדיו ראשונה/שנייה)** → אינפוגרפיקה גדולה ("איך מחשבים…") → **תוכן עניינים TOC עם 11 קישורי anchor** → 11 בלוקי H2 (מה זה מס רכישה, מדרגות 2026, ראשונה vs שנייה, קבלן, בניין, מסחרי, קרקע, מתנה, הנחות) → טבלאות מדרגות מעוצבות → CTA פנימי "צרו קשר עם יועץ"
- **מילים**: ~3,500 [crawled מקובץ הסריקה]
- **מדיה**: 1 אינפוגרפיקה SVG אחת ארוכה (לא צילומים)
- **Schema**: HowTo + FAQPage (לפי דפוס WP/Yoast הסטנדרטי שלהם)
- **Internal links UP**: הכוונה לעמוד הבית "נדלן מאסטר" + "השקעות נדלן בחו״ל"
- **Internal links DOWN**: למאמרים על מס שבח, היטל השבחה, חוק המכר
- **CTA placement**: כפתור "חישוב" סטטי בתוך המחשבון; אין sticky; טופס פנייה רק בפוטר

> **תובנה**: הם דחפו את המחשבון למעלה — וזה מה ש-Google מתגמל. ה-TOC הארוך עם anchor links מייצר Sitelinks ב-SERP.

#### B. `nadlanmaster.co.il/השקעות-נדלן-בחול/` — 13.32%
- **H1**: "השקעות נדלן בחו״ל – האם ואיפה להשקיע ב-2026?" (שאלה + שנה)
- **Block order**: H1 → אינטרו "חושבים להשקיע?…" (3 פסקאות עם רגש) → סימן ◄ ליתרונות → **TOC ידני עם asterisks ב-bold** (9 בלוקים) → 9 H2 (זמן טוב? יתרונות, מדינות מומלצות, פרויקטים, גרפי מחירים, בדיקות, שלבים, מיסוי, סיכונים) → גלריית כרטיסי פרויקטים (Wolfson 52, Nevo, Ramada Batumi, Havatzelet) עם תמונה+H4+לינק → טבלת מדינות → CTA "לפרויקטים נבחרים — לחצו כאן"
- **מילים**: ~7,500 [crawled]
- **Schema**: Article + ItemList (לכרטיסי פרויקטים)
- **Internal links DOWN**: 21 קישורים יוצאים לכל מדינה (קפריסין, פולין, גאורגיה, יוון, פורטוגל…)
- **Internal links UP**: ל"נדלן בישראל" ול"דירה להשקעה"
- **CTA**: anchor jump לקטע פרויקטים — חלש; אין lead form bound; פונים לטפסי פרויקט ספציפיים

> **תובנה**: pillar רחב משתמש כ-hub שמזרים PageRank ל-21 ספוקים של מדינות. שיטה שמשרתת גם SEO וגם affiliate.

#### C. `nadlanmaster.co.il/איפה-כדאי-לקנות-דירה-להשקעה/` — top page #4
- **H1**: שאלה + שנה ("איפה כדאי לקנות דירה להשקעה ב-2026?")
- **Block order**: H1 → "הקדמה קצרה" + נימוקי שוק → **TOC 8-bullet** → H2 הגדרה ("מה זה דירה להשקעה?") → השוואה לאפיקים אחרים → H3 תשואה ויציבות (עם דוגמה מספרית) → "הבנקים מעודדים" → **גלריית פרויקטים מומלצים** (4 כרטיסים) → טבלת ערים עם תשואות → איך מחשבים תשואה → מיסוי → FAQ ארוך
- **מילים**: ~6,000 [crawled]
- **CTA**: כרטיסי פרויקט = lead capture עקיף; אין lead form ראשי

#### D. `nadlanmaster.co.il/real-estate-prices/` — top page #5
- **H1**: "מגמת מחירי הנדל״ן בישראל ב-2026 — לאן הולך השוק?"
- **Block order**: H1 → 4 פסקאות bold כ-summary → אינפוגרפיקה → **TOC 15-bullet** → 15 H2 (תמונת מצב, מדידה, נתוני עשור, משמעות עליה, לפי מחוזות, גרף 5 שנים, סיבות, תחזית, השפעת המלחמה, ממוצע עליה, 20 שנה, חודשי, כמה משכורות לדירה, הון עצמי, החזר משכנתא) → טבלאות נתונים → לא טופס; CTA לקישורים פנימיים
- **מילים**: ~5,500
- **תובנה**: סדר מבוסס "נתון → פרשנות → השלכה" שעובד יפה ל-AI Overviews.

#### E. `madlan.co.il/area-info/<שכונה>` (Bat Galim וכו׳) [crawled — נתפס ע״י anti-bot 403]
- מבנה ידוע מציבורי: H1 = שם השכונה → סקור Madlan Score (1-10) → מפה אינטראקטיבית → גרף שינוי מחירים → טבלת עסקאות אחרונות מטאבו → דירוג בתי ספר/תחבורה/מטרדים → נכסים למכירה/השכרה בשכונה → "השאירו פרטים למתווך מקומי"
- **CTA**: lead form sticky במובייל
- **Schema**: Place + AggregateRating + BreadcrumbList + ItemList (נכסים)
- **תובנה**: זה הפורמט שגוגל אוהב לדפי "city/neighborhood intent" — חייבים לחקות *את המבנה*, לא את הטקסט.

---

### 2.2 ארבעה תבניות חוזרות לצוות התוכן

#### Template A — **Money Pillar** (למשל "מחשבון משכנתא", "מס רכישה", "דירה להשקעה")

```
1. H1 קצר exact-match (עד 6 מילים)
2. סאב-כותרת: 1 משפט "תועלת" + "מעודכן 2026"
3. INTERACTIVE TOOL (מחשבון/הדמיה) — חובה Above-the-fold
4. תקציר 3-bullet עם המספרים הקריטיים מתוך התוצאה
5. TOC anchor-links (10–15 פריטים) — חשוב ל-Sitelinks
6. 8–14 H2 לפי שאלות המשתמש (איך עובד / מדרגות / דירה ראשונה vs שנייה / פטור / איך חוסכים)
7. טבלת נתונים גדולה אחת לפחות (מדרגות / תשואות / החזרים)
8. אינפוגרפיקה SVG מקורית (לא stock)
9. בלוק "טעויות נפוצות" (3–5)
10. FAQ Schema-able (8–12)
11. Sticky CTA במובייל: "קבלו ייעוץ ראשוני" → טופס קצר 3 שדות
12. Internal links UP: pillar→hub
    Internal links DOWN: 8–15 spokes (כל ספוק → חזרה ל-pillar)
13. Author box + תאריך עדכון אחרון נראה
14. Schema: HowTo + FAQPage + BreadcrumbList + Article
```
**מילים יעד**: 2,500–4,000. **LCP יעד**: ≤2.5s גם עם המחשבון.

#### Template B — **City Hub** ("נדל״ן בתל אביב", "דירות בחיפה")

```
1. H1: "{עיר} — נדל״ן, מחירים ושכונות 2026"
2. Hero: מספר עסקאות אחרון + מחיר חציוני + שינוי YoY (3 KPI cards)
3. Map module: שכונות clickable (כל שכונה = לינק לדף area)
4. גרף מחיר חציוני 5 שנים (מ-nadlan.gov.il)
5. טבלת "10 העסקאות האחרונות" עם תאריך/כתובת/מ"ר/מחיר
6. רשימת שכונות עם Score (16–20 שכונות לערים גדולות)
7. "פרויקטים חדשים בעיר" — 6–8 כרטיסים (מתחבר לפרויקטים שלנו)
8. בלוק "כמה משכורות לדירה כאן" — calculator embed
9. 3–5 H2 editorial: למי מתאים לגור כאן / סיכויים להשקעה / תחבורה / חינוך
10. FAQ עירוני (5–8)
11. CTA: "בקש הערכת שווי לנכס שלך ב{עיר}"
12. Schema: Place + GeoCoordinates + AggregateRating + ItemList
```
**מילים יעד**: 1,800–2,500 (הנתונים עושים את העבודה, לא הטקסט).

#### Template C — **Project Landing** (יד שמאל לפרויקטים שאינם בבעלותנו ב-/city/.../projects/)

```
1. H1: "{שם פרויקט} — {עיר/שכונה}" + תג חי ("בשיווק"/"בבנייה"/"איכלוס")
2. Hero gallery (5–8 רנדרים/תמונות) + טריילר וידאו אופציונלי
3. Spec card sticky צד: יזם, מס׳ דירות, סוגי דירות, מועד אכלוס, אזור
4. טבלת מחירים פר חדרים (טווח)
5. תוכנית קומה + תוכנית טיפוסית (PDF)
6. מפה + רדיוס 1 ק"מ (בתי ספר, סופר, תחבורה)
7. השוואה ל-3 פרויקטים בסביבה (טבלת spec)
8. מחשבון "כמה משכנתא אצטרך" embed
9. בלוק יזם — תיק עבודות + פרויקטים קודמים שנמסרו (טאבו)
10. Lead form sticky desktop צד + bottom mobile
11. Schema: ApartmentComplex / Residence + Offer + LocalBusiness (יזם)
```
**מילים יעד**: 800–1,200 (זה דף transactional, לא essay).

#### Template D — **Guide / Spoke** ("חוק המכר", "תמ"א 38", "קבוצת רכישה")

```
1. H1: שאלה או מונח (תיאומי intent)
2. TL;DR בקופסת בולטים (3–5 בולטים) — עוזר ל-AI Overviews
3. תוכן עניינים
4. 6–10 H2 בסדר לוגי (מה זה → איך עובד → מי זכאי → דוגמאות → טעויות → צעדים → FAQ)
5. דוגמה מספרית אחת לפחות, כולה בטבלה
6. checklist להורדה (PDF גייט אופציונלי לליד)
7. שלושה pull-quotes / "חשוב לדעת" boxes
8. CTA "קבלו בדיקה משפטית" → pillar /real-estate-lawyer/ (כבר בבעלותנו)
9. Internal links UP: ל-pillar הרלוונטי (קנייה/מכירה/השקעה)
10. Schema: Article + FAQPage + HowTo (אם applicable)
```
**מילים יעד**: 1,200–2,000. **לעולם** לא שולח לעמוד הבית — שולח ל-pillar.

## 3. KEYWORD CLUSTERS BY MONEY PRIORITY

כל המספרים [Semrush IL] אלא אם מסומן [estimated].
ROI = (CPC × Volume) / KD — דירוג גס.

### 3.1 Cluster: **משכנתאות ומחשבונים** (BOFU/MOFU, money-makers)
| Keyword | Vol | KD | CPC | Intent | ריאלי 6ח | ריאלי 12ח |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| מחשבון משכנתא | 40,500 | 35 | $0.17 | comm-invest | ❌ | ⚠️ עם backlinks |
| משכנתא | 8,100 | 54 | $2.29 | comm | ❌ | ❌ |
| משכנתא הפוכה | 3,600 | 26 | $2.65 | comm | ⚠️ | ✅ |
| מס רכישה | 5,400 | 38 | $4.06 | comm-invest | ⚠️ | ✅ |
| מחשבון מס רכישה | 4,400 | 20 | $0 | tool | ✅ | ✅✅ |
| מחשבון תשואה | 2,400 | 43 | $1.74 | tool | ⚠️ | ✅ |
| מס שבח | 4,400 | 43 | $0.39 | comm-info | ❌ | ⚠️ |
| היטל השבחה | 3,600 | 31 | $0.62 | info | ⚠️ | ✅ |

> **התקפה ראשונה**: "מחשבון מס רכישה", "מחשבון תשואה", "משכנתא הפוכה". KD נמוך + CPC ≥$1 = ROI גבוה לדף חדש.

| Keyword | Vol | KD | CPC | Intent | 6ח | 12ח |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| נדלן | 6,600 | 50 | $1.92 | comm-brand | ❌ | ❌ |
| דירות למכירה | 4,400 | 50 | $1.26 | comm | ❌ | ❌ |

| מחירי דירות | 1,300 | 27 | $0.19 | info-comm | ⚠️ | ✅ |
| דירות יד שניה | 390 | 47 | $0.53 | comm | ❌ | ⚠️ |

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

## 5. HOMEPAGE STRUCTURE — מבנה "סמכות שקטה" לנד-לאן

(בהתבסס על מה שעובד אצל madlan + zillow + הפרשנות שלי. **לא** להעתיק מ-yad2 — דף בית של לוח לא מתאים לנו.)

```
[HEADER]
לוגו | חיפוש (חכם, autocomplete ערים+שכונות) | תפריט אופקי דק:
  קנייה ▾ | מכירה ▾ | השקעה ▾ | משכנתא ▾ | מסים ▾ | ערים ▾ | מדריכים ▾
  [כפתור משני]: "כניסה למקצוענים"

[HERO]
H1: "החלטות נדל״ן, על בסיס נתונים."
Sub: "מחשבונים, מדריכים ועסקאות אמת — לפני שאתם חותמים."
מודול חיפוש מרכזי, 3 טאבים: [למכירה] [להשכרה] [פרויקטים]
  שדה: עיר/שכונה (autocomplete מ-2,400 שכונות)
  טווח מחיר | חדרים | סוג נכס
  כפתור ראשי "חפש". מתחתיו מיקרו-לינק: "או בדוק שווי דירה →"
KPI strip קטן: "מבוסס נתוני רשות המסים · בנק ישראל · עודכן 2/יוני/2026"

[TRUST STRIP — שורה דקה אפורה]
לוגואים: רשות המסים | בנק ישראל | למ"ס | nadlan.gov.il

[SECTION 1: כלים — 6 כרטיסים]
H2: "החישובים שאסור לדלג עליהם"
6 כרטיסים (icon + שם + שורת תועלת + לינק):
  משכנתא | מס רכישה | קנייה vs שכירות | תשואה | הערכת שווי | Airbnb חו״ל
*** זה מה שכבר יש לכם — אבל הזיזו אותו ל-fold השני ועצבו נקי יותר ***

[SECTION 2: ערים — Grid 12 ערים]
H2: "השוק לפי עיר"
12 כרטיסי עיר (תל אביב, ירושלים, חיפה, ראשון, פ"ת, רמת גן, נתניה, חולון,
  באר שבע, אשדוד, רחובות, הרצליה). כל כרטיס: שם + מחיר חציוני + שינוי YoY + פס מיני-גרף.
לינק תחתון: "כל הערים (43) →"

[SECTION 3: עסקאות אחרונות — feed דינמי]
H2: "מה נסגר השבוע"
טבלה: עיר | רחוב (חלקי) | מ"ר | קומה | מחיר | תאריך
8-10 שורות חיות מ-nadlan.gov.il + לינק "מאגר עסקאות מלא →"
*** זה ה"data moat" — madlan עושה את זה בקטן, yad2 לא בכלל ***

[SECTION 4: מדריכי החלטה — 4 כרטיסים]
H2: "הידע שמגן על העסקה שלכם"
4 כרטיסים גדולים: קנייה ראשונה | מכירה | השקעה | מיסוי 2026
כל אחד: תמונה איכותית + intro + "התחל ←"

[SECTION 5: פרויקטים חדשים נבחרים (אם רלוונטי)]
H2: "פרויקטים שמעניינים השבוע"
6 כרטיסים (תמונה, שם, עיר, טווח מחיר, סטטוס)
*** רק אם יש לכם מודל אפיליאייט/פרסום ליזמים ***

[SECTION 6: שאלות נפוצות (FAQ Schema)]
6-8 שאלות-תשובות קצרות שמכוונות ל-People Also Ask:
  "מה זה מס רכישה?", "כמה הון עצמי צריך?", "תמ"א 38 — מי משלם?"…

[SECTION 7: Lead capture — *לא* טופס פסיבי]
H2: "צריכים בדיקה ראשונית?"
2-step form: [סוג העסקה] → [טלפון + שעה נוחה]
copy: "נחזור עם בדיקה ראשונית — 24 שעות. ללא עלות."

[FOOTER]
4 עמודות: על נד-לאן | מדריכים | כלים | משפטי
כתובת + טלפון + שעות מענה (E-A-T)
```

**מובייל**: hero → סרגל חיפוש sticky → 6 כלים → ערים grid (2 עמודות) → עסקאות (גלילה אופקית) → מדריכים → CTA → footer. Tab bar תחתון מתמיד עם 4 לשוניות (בית | חיפוש | כלים | חשבון).

**LCP יעד**: ≤2.0s; ה-Hero ללא תמונה כבדה — רק רקע גרדיאנט שקט וטיפוגרפיה.

---

## 6. PILLAR ARCHITECTURE + INTERNAL-LINK MODEL

### סילו (10 pillars):

```
1. PILLAR: רכישה               → /buying-apartment/
2. PILLAR: מכירה               → /selling-apartment/
3. PILLAR: השקעה               → /investment-apartment/
4. PILLAR: משכנתא              → /mortgage/                (חדש)
5. PILLAR: מיסוי               → /real-estate-tax-advisor/  (קיים)
6. PILLAR: עירוני (city hub)   → /cities/                   (חדש index)
7. PILLAR: שכונה (area hub)    → /neighborhoods/            (חדש index)
8. PILLAR: פרויקטים            → /projects/                 (חדש index)
9. PILLAR: התחדשות עירונית     → /urban-renewal/            (תמ"א 38, פינוי-בינוי)
10. PILLAR: מסחרי              → /commercial/               (משרדים, חנויות)
   (legal/lawyer pillar קיים — לא נכלל בהמלצות חדשות)
```

### עקרונות linking:
- **כל spoke מוכרח** לקשר UP אל ה-pillar שלו במשפט הראשון/השני.
- **כל pillar** מקשר DOWN ל-12–20 spokes (מאסטר תקרה: 25). nadlanmaster משתמש בקרוב ל-30 לינקים בחלק מהדפים — over-optimization, לא להעתיק.
- **Cross-silo**: רק דרך 1–2 "bridge links" בכל דף (מ-"דירה להשקעה" → "מחשבון משכנתא" → "מס רכישה"). לא ספאם.
- **Max internal links per page** [crawled מ-nadlanmaster]: ~80 כולל תפריט (sidebar שלהם דוחף 60+ ערים בכל עמוד — זה PageRank carpet אגרסיבי שעובד אבל מסריח). **המלצה לנו**: לא יותר מ-**40 לינקים פנימיים** בעמוד, כולל nav, ממוקדים.
- **Breadcrumbs Schema** חובה בכל pillar/spoke.
- **Sitemap**: 3 sitemaps נפרדים: `sitemap-pillars.xml`, `sitemap-cities.xml`, `sitemap-guides.xml`.

### דוגמה לסילו של "השקעה":

```
PILLAR: /investment-apartment/  (תוכן 3,000 מילים + מחשבון תשואה)
  ↓
  spoke: /investment-apartment/yield-calculation/
  spoke: /investment-apartment/where-to-invest-israel/
  spoke: /investment-apartment/cap-rate-explained/    ← Wiki gap
  spoke: /investment-apartment/cash-on-cash/          ← Wiki gap
  spoke: /investment-apartment/leverage-and-mortgage/
  spoke: /investment-apartment/airbnb-vs-long-term/
  spoke: /investment-apartment/foreign-vs-israel/
  spoke: /investment-apartment/reit-vs-direct/        ← Wiki gap
  spoke: /investment-apartment/crowdfunding/          ← Wiki gap
  spoke: /investment-apartment/dscr-explained/        ← Wiki gap
  bridge → /mortgage/calculator/
  bridge → /real-estate-tax-advisor/purchase-tax/
```

---

## 7. MONETIZATION MAP

| מתחרה | מקור הכנסה ראשי | ראיה on-page [crawled] |
|---|---|---|
| **yad2** | חבילות פרסום פרטיים + מתווכים + "פרויקט ממומן" + Banners | תגיות "מודעה ממומנת", "ניסיון Premium", banner Coral-Tell, פילטר "רק מתווכים מאומתים" |
| **madlan** | מנוי B2B למתווכים ("המערכת המקצועית"), pro data ליזמים, lead-routing | קישור top-nav "למעבר למערכת המקצועית", lead form פר אזור עם UTM למתווך |
| **nadlanmaster** | אפיליאייט/דמי הפניה ליזמים + פרסום פרויקט נקודתי | כרטיסי פרויקט עם פרטי קשר ספציפיים ליזם, UTM ב-outbound, "צרו קשר עם הפרויקט" |
| **nad-lan (אנחנו)** | היום: lead למשרד עו״ד/יועצים. אופציה: מחיר משתנה לפי איכות הליד | טופס "בדיקה ראשונית" → routing פנימי |

### אפיקי הכנסה לנד-לאן (לפי סדר ROI):
1. **Lead generation לעו"ד נדל"ן/יועצי משכנתאות** — כבר קיים. מחיר ליד ₪150–₪400 לפי איכות.
2. **חבילות "Pro Listing" ליזמים** בדפי `/cities/<city>/projects/` (אצלנו) — תג "מומלץ"/badge זהב.
3. **מנוי "Premium Insights" למשקיעים** — דוחות שכונה PDF, התראות חדש בעסקאות. ₪29–₪79/חודש.
4. **תוכן ממומן (sponsored capsules)** במאמרי pillar — שקוף ומסומן ("בחסות …").
5. **Affiliate למשכנתאות** (משכנתאמן וכו׳) — דרך מחשבון המשכנתא.
6. **API ל-data** (טווח ארוך) — sales B2B.

> **חשוב**: nadlanmaster מציג כרטיסי פרויקט בתוך מאמרי pillar (בלי disclosure ברור) — Google רואה את זה ובעידן SpamBrain זה התחיל לעלות במחיר. **אצלנו**: כל פלייסמנט ממומן יסומן `rel="sponsored"` ובלוק "בחסות" ויזואלי.

---

## 8. HONEST VERDICT + "BUILD-NOW" LIST

### חולשות וחוזקות:
- **yad2**: חזק — מלאי וזרימה. חלש — אפס תוכן editorial, אפס AI-Overview readiness, חוויית מובייל איטית.
- **madlan**: חזק — data moat, מפה, מותג. חלש — anti-bot אגרסיבי = AI engines (Perplexity, ChatGPT) לא קוראים אותם → הזדמנות ענקית עבורנו ב-Generative SERP.
- **nadlanmaster**: חזק — קלסטר תוכן, מחשבונים, אפיליאייט. חלש — UI ישן (תבנית The7), כתיבה ארוכה ומיושנת, אין data חי, יחס follow/nofollow רע (413 follow מתוך 5,191 = 8% בלבד).
- **nad-lan (אנחנו)**: חזק — 6 מחשבונים, מוכנות gov-data, branding נקי. חלש — אפס authority, אפס דפי ערים, lead form פסיבי, אין spoke-tree, דף בית רזה.

### 8 המהלכים בעלי המינוף הגבוה ביותר (לעשות עכשיו, בסדר):

1. **בנו 8 דפי כלים כ-pillars אמיתיים** (לא רק מחשבון בודד) — הוסיפו 2,500 מילים, TOC, FAQ, HowTo schema. התחילו מ-`mortgage-calculator/`, `purchase-tax-calculator/`, `investment-property-cashflow-calculator/`. **תוך 90 יום זה לבד ייתן 5K-10K visits/חודש**.
2. **כתבו 25 מאמרי "Wikipedia void"** (מהרשימה בסעיף 4). KD<10, kompetition אפס. כל אחד מקושר UP ל-pillar שלו. **6 שבועות, content sprint**.
3. **השיקו 12 city hubs** (תל אביב, ירושלים, חיפה, ראשון, פ"ת, רמת גן, נתניה, חולון, באר שבע, אשדוד, רחובות, הרצליה) לפי Template B — KPI cards + מפה + עסקאות חיות מ-nadlan.gov.il + 4–6 שכונות לכל עיר. **זה הקטר ל"מחירי דירות ב{עיר}"**.
4. **שדרגו דף הבית** לפי סעיף 5 — תזיזו KPI cards ועסקאות חיות גבוה. ה-hero של היום בעיקר "כלים" — חסר הוכחות אמת.
5. **תשתית Schema מלאה**: Article + FAQ + HowTo + BreadcrumbList + Place + Calculator (SoftwareApplication). madlan ו-yad2 חלשים פה — נכנס יפה ל-AI Overviews.
6. **Sticky lead form במובייל** בכל מאמר pillar — 2-step (סוג עסקה → טלפון). הטופס היחיד שלכם היום בתחתית — פספוס.
7. **קמפיין backlinks ב-3 כיוונים**: (א) PR ל-themarker/calcalist על נתון "כמה משכורות לדירה בכל עיר" שתפרסמו (linkbait מתוצר משלכם). (ב) חברות משכנתא ויועצים שיקשרו למחשבון. (ג) צמיתות עירוניות — קישור מאתרי עיריות ל-city hubs ("מידע על השכונה ב…").
8. **AI/LLM optimization**: שמרו על HTML נקי (לא JS-rendered), TL;DR בולטים, נתונים בטבלאות עם `<caption>`, FAQ עם ראיון-תשובה ברור. **madlan חוסם, yad2 בלבול** → אנחנו יכולים להפוך למקור #1 ש-ChatGPT/Perplexity יצטטו על נדל"ן ישראלי תוך 6 חודשים.

### מה לא לעשות:
- ❌ אל תרדפו "דירות למכירה" / "דירות להשכרה" — לא תנצחו את yad2/madlan, ולא תרוויחו.
- ❌ אל תבזבזו על "נדלן בחו"ל" עכשיו (פאזה 3).
- ❌ אל תעתיקו את הניווט-קרפט של nadlanmaster (60 ערים בכל פוטר) — Google מענישה ב-2026.
- ❌ אל תכניסו popups, exit-intent, או אוטופליי — שובר את הבראנד "סמכות שקטה".

---

## HONESTY STATEMENT

**מה שאומת חי [crawled היום]:**
- HTML של nad-lan.co.il, nadlanmaster.co.il (homepage + 4 דפים top), ניסיון של madlan.co.il (חסם anti-bot 403 — שלד מבני מתבסס על ידע ציבורי + תוצאות Semrush).
- Semrush IL: domain overview, top pages, backlink profile, keyword compare ל-25 מילים.

**מה הוסק / הוערך [estimated]:**
- חלק מ-search volumes ו-KD למילים שלא היו ב-batch של 25 ה-keyword_compare.
- מבנה דפי madlan area-info — הוסק מ-public knowledge + ה-URLs שכן הופיעו ב-top_pages, ולא מסריקת HTML מלאה (חסום).
- ספירת Wikipedia gaps: השוויתי שמות-מונחים מקבילים — צוות התוכן יאמת קיום/אי-קיום של כל ערך ויקיפדיה לפני פרסום.
- אומדן מחירי לידים (₪150–₪400), CPM של פרסום ליזמים, מחירי מנוי B2B — לא חולצו ממקור חי; הם רף שוק סביר ב-IL מקצב 2024-2026 ויש לאמת מול sales.

**מה דורש Semrush בתשלום / Search Console:**
- KD מדויק לכל מילה (יש לי רק ל-25 מילים שעברו דרך keyword_compare).
- היסטוריית דירוגים של nadlanmaster ו-madlan (Position Tracking).
- Backlink gap analysis מלא בין nad-lan לבין שני המתחרים (Backlinks Audit).
- CTR אמיתי של דפי המתחרים — לא זמין ציבורית.

**מה דורש כניסה לאתרים פיזית / API:**
- ספירת lead forms / conversions אמיתית של המתחרים — בלתי אפשרי בלי גישה לדאשבורד שלהם.
- מבנה ה-data של madlan (Madlan Score, gov data ingestion) — דורש reverse-engineering ל-network requests.

**מה לא נעשה והומלץ לבעלי המוצר:**
- בדיקת LCP/CWV של דפי המתחרים בכלי בודד (PageSpeed Insights) — קל לבצע ידנית, לא הופעל פה.
- ניתוח Search Console של nad-lan עצמו (queries, impressions, CTR) — צריך גישה לחשבון שלכם.

מסמך זה נשמר ב: `/mnt/documents/nadlan-competitor-blueprint.md`

# Luxury Design Language — nad-lan.co.il

> **Notice to all agents:** this is the canonical visual system, derived 2026-05-28 from studying the most premium real-estate websites in the world (Sotheby's International Realty, Christie's International Real Estate, The Agency, Compass, and the Mediaboom "50 luxury examples" + Luxury Presence typography guidance). It **supersedes** the earlier `visual-design-skill.md` corporate-blue palette. When the two conflict, this file wins. The owner rejected the corporate-blue/trust-blue look and the cartoon-house logo on 2026-05-28 and asked explicitly to copy the luxury leaders' design language.

## Sources studied (proof of research, 2026-05-28)

- Sotheby's International Realty — `sothebysrealty.com` — centuries-of-tradition luxury, serif-led, restrained.
- Christie's International Real Estate — `christiesrealestate.com` — "Parisian interiors, streamlined minimalist layout, exclusivity."
- The Agency — `theagencyre.com` — "minimalist design + impactful full-bleed visuals, immersive."
- Compass — clean modern, high-quality imagery, straightforward search.
- Mediaboom "50 Luxury Real Estate Website Design" — concrete patterns (below).
- Luxury Presence brand-fonts guide — typography consensus (Playfair Display serif headings).

## The distilled luxury design language

### Principle: restraint + warmth + space
Luxury is **forced minimalism**. Generous whitespace signals confidence. Content (and eventually photography) does the talking. Never loud, never busy, never bright-corporate.

### Typography — THE single biggest lever
Research consensus: **serif headings + light sans body** = expensive. Sans-only = affordable/cheap.

- **Headings: Frank Ruhl Libre** — the Hebrew transitional serif, the Hebrew counterpart to Playfair Display. Elegant, traditional, authoritative. Weights 500/700/900. Bundled at `assets/fonts/frank-ruhl-libre/`.
- **Body: Heebo** — clean humanist sans, weight 300–400 for that light luxury feel. Bundled at `assets/fonts/heebo/`.
- **Eyebrows / labels / categories:** Heebo, small (12–13px), **wide letter-spacing** (0.15–0.2em), often with manual character spacing in Hebrew (no uppercase exists in Hebrew, so tracking carries the "label" signal).
- Big serif headings get **tight** tracking (-0.01 to -0.02em) and generous line-height (1.15–1.25).
- Body: 17–18px, line-height 1.7 (more air than the earlier 1.65).
- **No font bundled from a CDN at runtime** — all woff2 local in the theme (page-speed + privacy + no external dependency).

### Color — warm minimal, NOT corporate blue
Pivot away from `#0E3A8A` trust-blue. Luxury real estate = warm neutrals + one restrained metallic accent.

| Token | Hex | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `ink` (contrast) | `#1A1A1C` | near-black text + dark sections (not pure #000) |
| `base` | `#FFFFFF` | page background |
| `cream` | `#F7F4ED` | warm section background, cards, panels |
| `gold` (accent) | `#B08D57` | the single metallic accent — rules, small highlights, hovers. Muted antique gold, NOT bright #D89B3C |
| `stone` | `#7A746C` | muted secondary text, eyebrows, captions |
| `line` | `#E5E1D8` | hairline borders, dividers (warm, not cool gray) |
| `positive` | `#3E6F57` | price-up (muted forest, not bright green) |
| `negative` | `#9B4A3F` | price-down (muted terracotta, not bright red) |

Rule: **two neutrals (cream + ink) + one accent (gold)**. Changing colors per section = cheap. Stick to these everywhere.

### Whitespace & rhythm
- Section vertical padding: 96–140px desktop, 64–80px mobile (more than the earlier 80/64).
- Max content width ~720px for text, ~1280px for wide sections.
- Card padding 28–32px.
- Let single elements stand alone with air around them.

### Shadows — subtle, warm, layered (NOT heavy)
- Default card: none or `0 1px 2px rgba(26,26,28,.04)`.
- Hover lift: `0 12px 32px rgba(26,26,28,.10)` + translateY(-3px), 200ms ease.
- 2026 luxury leans toward **hairline borders over shadows** (Tactile-Brutalism-lite). Prefer a 1px `line` border + tiny shadow over big soft shadows.

### Buttons — quiet, not loud
- Primary: ink background, cream text, **no border-radius or tiny (2–4px)**, generous padding (16px × 36px), small letter-spaced label. Hover: gold background OR subtle lift.
- Secondary: transparent with 1px ink/gold border, ink text. Hover: fill.
- **No bright fills, no big rounded pills, no gradients.** Luxury buttons are restrained rectangles.

### Header — thin, sticky, minimal
- Slim (64–72px), cream or white, 1px bottom hairline.
- Serif or refined wordmark logo (right, RTL).
- Minimal nav, wide-tracked.
- Sticky / "traveling" on scroll with a subtle backdrop blur + hairline.
- A single quiet CTA, not a loud button.

### Logo
- **Elegant serif wordmark** in Frank Ruhl Libre — "נדל״ן חכם" — with a thin gold rule and a wide-tracked tagline eyebrow.
- **No cartoon house icon** (rejected by owner). If a mark is used, a refined monogram (נ) in a thin gold frame, like a crest.
- Favicon: monogram "נ" Frank Ruhl on ink with thin gold inner frame.

### Imagery (when available, Codex)
- Full-bleed, high-resolution architectural photography.
- Matte, not over-saturated. Warm tone to match cream palette.
- Generous whitespace around images, never cramped.

### Micro-interactions (systematic, not ad-hoc)
- Global tokens: transition 200ms, ease `cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1)`.
- Link hover: animated underline (gold), grows from right (RTL).
- Card hover: lift 3px + hairline → soft shadow.
- Button hover: bg shift to gold or ink↔cream invert.
- Smooth scroll, antialiased font smoothing, branded text selection (cream-on-gold).

## Implementation map

- **theme.json** — palette (above), fontFamilies with local fontFace for Frank Ruhl Libre + Heebo, heading styles (serif, tracking), button styles (quiet), spacing scale, body 1.7 line-height. Needs theme re-sync.
- **style.css + style.min.css** — micro-interactions, sticky header polish, link underline animation, card hover, selection color, eyebrow letter-spacing utility. Needs theme re-sync.
- **Logo / favicon / OG** — generated PNGs in `assets/branding/`, uploaded to media, set as site_logo + site_icon + Yoast default OG. Live via REST, no sync.
- **Header/footer template parts** — refine to thin sticky header + restrained footer. Live via REST.

## What this replaces / deprecates
- `visual-design-skill.md` §6.1 palette (gold #D89B3C bright, trust blue dominant) → DEPRECATED in favor of the warm-minimal palette here.
- The "green-dominant" accidental look from Codex's accent-3 usage → overridden by the new palette.
- The cartoon-house logo (v1) → replaced by serif wordmark.

## Open TODOs
- [ ] Owner approves the luxury logo/OG direction (sent 2026-05-28).
- [ ] After approval: owner does ONE theme re-sync (UPress Git pull from main) to apply theme.json + bundled fonts + premium CSS in one shot.
- [ ] Re-upload approved logo/favicon/OG (already wired; swap media if direction changes).
- [ ] Refine header template part to thin sticky + serif wordmark.
- [ ] Codex: source warm architectural photography per image-pipeline.md.
- [ ] Consider a serif-headings + photography hero on the homepage (Codex content).

### skill: Luxury Design System — Master skill
**→ skills/luxury-design-system.md**


> **Notice to all agents:** This is the **canonical visual system** for nad-lan.co.il. It is derived from Lovable's design output (`docs/design/lovable-output-2026-05-28.md`), which is the **source of truth**. This skill summarises rules and operational decisions; when in doubt, open the source doc. This file **supersedes** the earlier `visual-design-skill.md` palette/type AND the provisional `luxury-design-language.md`. They are deprecated.

## Read order for a designer/dev agent

1. This file (master rules + tokens)
2. `docs/design/lovable-output-2026-05-28.md` (the full design system from Lovable: competitor DNA, all tokens, all screens, all components, honesty statement)
3. `skills/design-page-patterns.md` (how each page type is built)
4. `skills/design-components.md` (every component, every state)
5. `skills/design-micro-interactions.md` (motion matrix)
6. `skills/design-logo-mark.md` (logo lockups)
7. `skills/design-rtl-hebrew.md` (RTL-specific rules pulled out for emphasis)

## What this system IS

A calm, serif-led, warm-cream RTL Hebrew platform where tools and knowledge are presented with the dignity of a **private bank's research desk** — never as a marketplace, never as a SaaS dashboard, never as a listings board. Synthesis of Sotheby's typographic restraint, Christie's Parisian silence + warm cream palette, The Agency's confidence to leave a hero alone, Compass's utility-as-hero clarity, Luxury Presence's asymmetric editorial grid for guides.

## Hard rules — never break these

1. **Two neutrals + one accent.** Two neutrals are cream + ink; the single accent is muted antique gold. Never two saturated colors on one surface. Never bright colors.
2. **Gold is for ≤5% of any screen.** Never two saturated colors on the same surface.
3. **Serif headings (Frank Ruhl Libre) + light sans body (Heebo).** Heading weight is **500**, never 700 (bold Hebrew serif looks dense). Body weight ≤500.
4. **Hairlines over shadows.** Default to 1px borders. When shadow is used: warm, low, never blue. ≤1 elevated layer visible at once on a section.
5. **Radii: 0–2px on everything.** Only avatars and filter chips get pill radius. Images get 0px.
6. **Pure black and pure white are forbidden** outside of paper cards (paper-0 = #FFF is allowed only on card surfaces).
7. **Positive/negative tones appear only inside numeric data**, never as decorative chrome.
8. **No dark mode.** Lovable's self-critique fix #4: dark mode cheapens warm-cream systems.
9. **All transitions respect `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`** → durations 0, no transforms.
10. **RTL-first.** Every directional decision (underline origin, table numeric edge, drawer side, breadcrumb separator, next/prev arrows) is RTL-aware. See `design-rtl-hebrew.md`.

## Tokens (the values to paste into theme.json + style.css)

### Color (WCAG-AA verified against intended bg per Lovable)
```
--ink-900:       #1B1A17   text, headings, primary button fill
--ink-700:       #2E2B26   strong UI text
--ink-500:       #5C564D   secondary text, captions
--stone-400:     #8A8276   tertiary text, metadata, placeholders
--stone-200:     #C9C3B7   disabled, muted icons
--cream-50:      #FAF7F1   page background (primary)
--cream-100:    #F3EEE3   section alt background, table stripe
--paper-0:       #FFFFFF   card surface, input fill ONLY
--gold-600:      #9C7A3C   accent — links, focus ring, rule, monogram frame
--gold-500:      #B89154   accent hover, chart accent
--gold-200:     #E6D4AE   soft accent wash (used <5%)
--hairline:      #E2DCD0   1px borders, dividers, table grid
--hairline-strong:#C9C0AE  stronger divider, input border
--positive-700:  #3F6B4A   price-up (muted forest)
--negative-700:  #8B3A2E   price-down (muted terracotta)
--focus-ring:    rgba(156,122,60,.40)
--overlay-ink:   rgba(27,26,23,.55)
```

### Typography
- Heading family: `"Frank Ruhl Libre", "Cormorant Garamond", Georgia, serif` — self-hosted woff2 at `assets/fonts/frank-ruhl-libre/`
- Body family: `"Heebo", "Inter", system-ui, sans-serif` — self-hosted woff2 at `assets/fonts/heebo/`
- **Full type scale (desktop + mobile) lives in `docs/design/lovable-output-2026-05-28.md` §Typography.** Mandatory tokens: `display-1, display-2, h1, h2, h3, h4, body-lg, body, body-sm, caption, eyebrow, micro, quote`.
- Article reading column: **680px max** (60–75 Hebrew chars/line).
- Tabular numerics in tools/tables only: `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums lining-nums`.
- Latin digits inside Hebrew remain LTR — wrap any digit run in `<bdi>` to preserve order.

### Spacing
Base unit **8px**. Scale `4, 8, 12, 16, 20, 24, 32, 40, 48, 64, 80, 96, 128, 160` → `--space-1`..`--space-14`. Section vertical rhythm: desktop **96px** top/bottom, mobile **64px**. Inter-block within a section: **40px** desktop / **32px** mobile.

### Radius / Shadow / Motion / Z-index
All values in source doc + `design-components.md`. Highlights: radius is 0/2/4/pill only; shadows are subtle warm `0 1px 2px / 0 2px 4px+8px 24px / 0 12px 40px` ink-tinted; motion uses 120/220/360/600ms with `cubic-bezier(.2,.6,.2,1)` standard.

### Grid
12 columns, **24px gutter** desktop, **16px** mobile. Container max **1280px** with **80px** outer margin desktop / **20px** mobile. Article reading column **680px**. Tools column **920px**.

## Implementation map (where each token lives)

| Token category | WordPress location |
|---|---|
| Color palette + slugs | `theme.json` → `settings.color.palette` |
| Font families + woff2 fontFace | `theme.json` → `settings.typography.fontFamilies` |
| Font sizes (display-1..micro) | `theme.json` → `settings.typography.fontSizes` |
| Spacing scale | `theme.json` → `settings.spacing.spacingSizes` |
| Shadows | `theme.json` → `settings.shadow.presets` |
| Heading element styles | `theme.json` → `styles.elements.h1..h6` |
| Button defaults | `theme.json` → `styles.elements.button` |
| Component class CSS (.card, .btn, .input-underline, etc.) | `style.css` + `style.min.css` |
| Micro-interactions / focus rings / animated underlines | `style.css` |
| Logo, favicon, OG | Media library; `site_logo`, `site_icon` via `wp/v2/settings`; Yoast Search Appearance for OG |

## Deprecations enacted by this skill

- **`skills/visual-design-skill.md` §6 palette + §6.2 typography**: deprecated. The bright `#D89B3C` gold and `#0E3A8A` trust blue are NOT the brand. Use the tokens above.
- **`skills/luxury-design-language.md` provisional palette**: superseded by Lovable's WCAG-verified palette above.
- **Live theme.json color slugs** (`accent-1`..`accent-6` from the Twenty Twenty-Five fork): **must be remapped** to the new scale on the next theme update. The current calculator widgets and footer use the deprecated palette (`#0E3A8A`, `#D89B3C`, `#0E7C57`) — they need a retoken pass.
- **Calculator widget styling** (`assets/widgets/mortgage-calculator.html`, `assets/widgets/purchase-tax-calculator.html`): inline `<style>` uses the deprecated palette + corporate blue gradient on the result tile. Retoken to the new system per `design-components.md` and per Lovable's calculator screen spec.
- **Premium footer** (`docs/wp-state/template-part-footer.html` deployed live as `custom` override): uses `contrast` (dark) bg with `accent-1` gold — gold is correct slug, but values must remap. Footer **structurally** matches Lovable's footer spec; only the palette+typography refresh remain.

## Open TODOs

- [ ] Owner runs the **second Lovable prompt** (committed at `docs/lovable-prompt-2.md`) to fill the 5 documented gaps.
- [ ] Port Lovable's tokens into `theme.json` (palette + fontFamilies with fontFace + fontSizes + spacingSizes + shadow presets + element styles).
- [ ] Port Lovable's components into `style.css` (and `style.min.css` — the theme enqueues `.min` in production).
- [ ] Rebuild the calculator widget `<style>` blocks to the new tokens + Lovable's calculator spec.
- [ ] Regenerate logo PNG/SVG to Lovable's monogram-in-double-circle spec (the cartoon-house logo currently set as `site_logo` is rejected; replace).
- [ ] Replace the OG image with Lovable's editorial spec.
- [ ] Translate Lovable's section blueprints to Gutenberg `<!-- wp:* -->` block patterns (after second prompt — Lovable's pattern markup will be in the gap fill).

### skill: Monetization — the lawyer-owner angle
**→ skills/monetization-lawyer-angle.md**


> **Notice to all agents:** the owner of nad-lan.co.il is a **practicing Israeli lawyer (עורך דין)**, not a real estate broker. This single fact changes the entire monetization model. If you are about to design a revenue stream that ignores this, stop and re-read.

## Why this matters more than any keyword

In Israel, every real-estate transaction of meaningful size involves at least one lawyer per side. Buyer's lawyer fee is typically **0.5%-1% of deal value** (market estimate, varies — owner can confirm). On a 2.5M ₪ apartment that is **12,500-25,000 ₪ per closed deal**, for one side. The owner can be that lawyer.

This makes the website not a media business that sells ads. It is a **client-acquisition engine for the lawyer's practice**, where:
- Content drives organic traffic on real-estate intent keywords.
- Calculators and forms capture the visitor's intent and contact details.
- The lawyer-owner (and approved associates) handle the legal closing.
- Side revenue from partner referrals (mortgage, appraisal, broker, inspection) layers on top.

Per closed deal in this model, the gross revenue is **10x-100x** what an agent-subscription site can extract from the same visitor.

## Re-ranked revenue tiers

### Tier A — primary (deal-driven, owner-captured)

**A1. Closing-attorney work generated by the site.**
- Buyer representation: 0.5-1% of deal value (market estimate, confirm).
- Seller representation: 0.5-1% of deal value (market estimate, confirm).
- Real volume comes from inquiry-form leads on `/buying/`, `/selling/`, `/investment/`, `/tax-legal/`, and all city/neighborhood pages.
- **Open task: define the lead → intake → engagement-letter flow.** Inquiry forms cannot collect privileged information; first intake must be a phone call.

**A2. Urban-renewal representation (tama 38, פינוי-בינוי).**
- Owner can represent the **residents' committee** (ועד הדיירים) in negotiations with a developer. Fees vary: flat retainer + per-apartment closing fee, or % of value uplift.
- Single building can be a 6-7 figure NIS engagement spread over years.
- The `/urban-renewal/` pillar should funnel directly to this practice.

**A3. Investor representation, especially foreign-Jewish buyers (US/France/UK).**
- High-ticket deals (often >3M ₪), often non-Hebrew-speaking client, often need lawyer to coordinate with bank, tax authority, accountant.
- Premium fees. Premium clients. Underserved by Hebrew-only law firms.
- Strategic opening: an English (and possibly French) microsite under `/en/` or a subdomain, marketed via the investor pillar.

**A4. Tax representation / tax-planning.**
- Pre-deal: planning purchase tax exposure, Israeli/foreign tax interaction, מס שבח planning before sale, exemptions.
- Owner is a lawyer — can structure this as a paid consultation (flat fee per case) plus implementation.

### Tier B — referral / pay-per-lead (partner-driven)

**B1. Mortgage broker referrals.**
- Per-lead pricing in Israel for qualified mortgage broker leads: roughly **150-400 ₪/lead** (market estimate, confirm by calling 2-3 brokers).
- Referral source: `/mortgage/` pillar and the mortgage calculator (the #1 traffic wedge per SERP analysis).
- Legally clean as long as the referral is disclosed and the owner does not act as a financial advisor.

**B2. Appraisal services.**
- Per-lead or revenue share with a partner appraiser.
- Referral source: `/tools/valuation/` and the "valuation report" CTA on city/neighborhood pages.

**B3. Real-estate broker (מתווך) referrals.**
- Per Israeli broker law (חוק המתווכים, 1996), only licensed brokers can charge "broker fees" (דמי תיווך). The owner is NOT a broker. The site CANNOT charge broker fees.
- **What it CAN do:** charge a finder's fee (דמי איתור) or pay-per-lead from a licensed broker for sending them a qualified buyer or seller. Must be structured as a paid referral / advertising service, not as brokerage. The lawyer-owner is well-positioned to draft this contract correctly.
- Per-deal closed broker-side commission in Israel is typically ~2% per side (market estimate). A 10-20% referral fee = **4,000-10,000 ₪ per closed deal**.

**B4. Developer (יזם) project advertising.**
- A single new-project marketing campaign with a major Israeli platform reportedly pays **20,000-80,000 ₪/month** (market estimate, directional, confirm with a sales conversation).
- Realistic only once nad-lan has provable traffic in the project's target city. Not a Year 1 revenue stream.
- The `/projects/{slug}/` template is built to receive these.

**B5. Home inspection (בדק בית) referrals.**
- Per-lead pricing exists but small. Layer onto the buying funnel.

### Tier C — owned products (medium-term)

**C1. Paid neighborhood reports (PDF).**
- A 20-page detailed report on a specific neighborhood: transaction trends, price/sqm by room count, school catchment, transit accessibility, planning pipeline.
- Sold for, e.g., 79-149 ₪. Or distributed free in exchange for email + phone (lead magnet for Tier A).

**C2. Paid courses (תמא 38 לדיירים, השקעה בנדל"ן, מס שבח למוכרים).**
- Owner can teach as לאחר רישיון (continuing-ed style). Underserved Hebrew market.
- Medium-term, requires content production and a course platform.

**C3. Paid pre-deal review.**
- Flat fee for reviewing a draft contract before the client closes elsewhere. Funnels to A1 in many cases.

### Tier D — last (only if scale arrives)

**D1. Display ads / programmatic.** Skip unless traffic is huge; degrades brand.
**D2. Affiliate links** (banks, insurance). Token revenue, distracting from Tier A.

## What changes in the architecture because of this

| Element | Before (broker-platform model) | Now (lawyer-owner model) |
|---|---|---|
| Hero by-line | none | "נכתב ומלווה משפטית על-ידי עו"ד [שם]" |
| Lead form fields | name, phone, city | + property type (buying/selling/investing/inheriting), urgency, deal stage |
| Default CTA | "השאירו פרטים" | "ייעוץ ראשוני בחינם — שיחה של 15 דקות" |
| Author of legal/tax pages | generic / none | the lawyer (Person schema) |
| Trust strip | inventory count | inventory + לשכת עו"ד registration |
| Footer | standard | + lawyer disclosure + bar number |
| `professional` CPT featured rank | paid agents top | **the owner's law practice top, others below** |
| Lead routing | round-robin to paying agents | **routed first to the law practice intake, only overflow to partners** |

## Israeli legal-marketing constraints (must respect)

The Israeli Bar (לשכת עורכי הדין) imposes restrictions on lawyer advertising. The owner knows these better than any agent — **defer to the owner** on:

- What can appear in by-line ("עו"ד" + name + bar number: OK).
- Claims of expertise (use "מתמחה ב-" cautiously; certain specializations are regulated terms).
- "Free consultation" / "ייעוץ חינם" wording — typically allowed for an initial conversation, but check.
- Testimonials / reviews — restricted; do not solicit publicly.
- Comparative claims about other lawyers — prohibited.
- Joint ventures with non-lawyers (like a broker) — restricted; do not draft any such partnership without the owner's explicit instruction.

**Action:** before any public-facing lawyer-marketing copy ships, route through the owner. This is one of those "out of scope for autonomous action" items (`agent-coordination-protocol.md`).

## Year 1 revenue targets (illustrative, not committed)

Setting these for orientation only. Owner to confirm or override.

- Month 1-3: Site built, GSC + Yoast configured. Zero revenue.
- Month 4-6: First organic traffic on the mortgage calculator + מס רכישה pillar. 1-3 qualified inquiries/month to the law practice. ~5K-15K ₪/month.
- Month 7-12: Pillars maturing. 10-20 qualified inquiries/month. 1-3 closed deals/month → 25K-75K ₪/month. Mortgage referral revenue beginning.
- Year 2+: Compounding organic + brand. The investor and tama-38 verticals become primary.

## What we do NOT do for revenue

- Sell traffic data to third parties.
- Display intrusive interstitials.
- Run lead-sale "auctions" between competing lawyers (regulatory landmine).
- Publish bait content with no E-E-A-T (kills the legal brand long-term).
- Charge users any fee to view content. Content is the magnet.

## Open TODOs for next agent

- [ ] Owner: confirm actual buyer-attorney and seller-attorney fee range used in the firm. Replace "0.5-1%" placeholders.
- [ ] Owner: confirm Israeli Bar permitted wording for "free initial consultation" and update CTAs in `copywriting-skill.md`.
- [ ] Owner: decide on English/French microsite for foreign-Jewish investors. Big strategic call; affects Yoast, schema, hreflang.
- [ ] Next Claude session: design the inquiry-form → law-firm intake CRM flow without exposing internals on public pages.

### skill: Skill: Monetization, Ad-Sales & Business Readiness (real-estate content site)
**→ skills/monetization-readiness-and-adsales.md**


> The honest playbook for selling advertising/listings/sponsorship on nad-lan.co.il and delivering real value. Built from cited competitor research (global + Israel) + a verified audit of what the site has today. Core principle: **never sell exposure we can't measure or an outcome we can't control. Sell a verifiable artifact, a guaranteed position/duration, or a pay-per-result.**

## A. What competitors actually deliver for money (the bar)

**Global (what the buyer GETS, and the guarantee):**
- **Zillow Premier Agent** = share-of-voice impression ads by ZIP. Official terms **explicitly disclaim any impression or lead guarantee** ("as is"). Reporting = lead dashboard + answer-rate. Lesson: even the leader sells best-efforts visibility, legally hedged — only sustainable because of traffic dominance.
- **Rightmove (UK)** = listing-depth upgrades. **Featured = guaranteed top-of-results for the period** (the cleanest "guaranteed deliverable"). Premium = 3 photos + highlight; claims +35% brochure views. Every membership includes a **trackable phone number + call recording** (attribution). Agency ARPA £1,530; growth is from upselling MORE products per agent.
- **REA / realestate.com.au** = depth ladder (Feature→Highlight→Premiere→Luxe), guaranteed relative position by tier. Audience Maximiser recently moved **impressions→clicks** (pay for a measurable action). Ignite dashboard = real-time views/enquiries/saves + competitor market-share.
- **Apartments.com/CoStar** = tiers with exposure multipliers (Diamond +1000% vs Silver) — but footnoted as **historical averages, not guarantees**. Deliverables include analytics portal + call recording + photo/3D specs per tier.
- **Redfin / Realtor.com ReadyConnect** = **pay-at-close referral** (~30-35% of commission). The only truly outcome-tied model; guarantees a referral, not a sale, policed by response-time SLAs.
- **Content/advice sites (Bankrate/NerdWallet/Houzz)** = performance affiliate (CPL/CPA) + display + sponsored. Houzz sells flat-monthly exposure, not guaranteed leads.

**Israel (cited ₪, pre-VAT):**
- **Free private listing** is standard (Yad2/Madlan/Homeless/Komo). Paid promotion = highlight + top placement + auto-bump for a fixed window. **WinWin benchmark: ₪49-99**, duration 60-180 days. **Komo PPV: ~₪0.50/view floor.** Yad2/Madlan current promo prices are **sales-gated/unpublished**.
- **Broker subscriptions:** unpublished; TheMarker reports Yad2 raised broker prices "tens of percent." Brokers must buy a package (can't post private).
- **Developer (Yad1):** project **mini-site + 3D tours + cross-placement + lead feed**, "packages by project type," price on inquiry. Pitch = ~4M entries / 1.6M visitors / 650K project-views monthly.
- **Sponsored content (best-documented):** TheMarker **₪15,000** (client writes) / **₪18,000** (they write), with a **guaranteed minimum 1,500 unique visitors** + Friday print. Walla ~₪4,200, Calcalist ~₪2,000, Reshet13 ~₪1,500. Niche real-estate site realistic band: **₪350-3,000/article**.
- **Leads (cited price list, ex-VAT):** new apartment domestic **₪90-120**, foreign **₪100-140**; investment **₪80-140**; mortgage **₪50-80**; lawyers **₪40-80**; renovation **₪50-200**. Exclusive leads top of range.
- **Banner CPM (Israeli digital benchmark):** **₪4-20 CPM**.

**The cross-cutting lesson:** the products that DON'T generate "I paid, what did I get?" complaints are: (1) a **verifiable asset** (a page/profile that exists regardless of traffic), (2) **guaranteed position for a fixed duration**, (3) a **reporting dashboard/recorded calls**, or (4) **pay-per-result** (lead/close). The fragile product is undifferentiated impression/"exposure."

## B. What nad-lan has TODAY (verified audit 2026-05-31)
- ✅ WooCommerce SKUs + prices: Pro directory (₪299/349/749), promoted property, developer campaign (₪10,990/19,990), a free tier. Payment wired (Grow/Bit/Stripe). `/join-pro/` sales page live.
- ✅ Catalog CPTs + archives: `nadlan_property` (5 seed), `nadlan_project` (**0**), `nadlan_professional` (**0**). Lead REST endpoint + lead CPT.
- ❌ **NO analytics installed** (no GA4, no GTM ID, no Search Console, no Bing) — cannot measure or prove traffic.
- ❌ **No traffic** (new, unranked). ❌ Directory empty. ❌ No `/advertise/` or `/media-kit/` page. ❌ `/pricing/` slug collided into an article.

## C. The product ladder — what we can sell HONESTLY, by phase

**Customer types & their artifact:**
1. **Real-estate professionals** (agents, lawyers, mortgage advisors, appraisers) → directory profile (Basic/Pro/Premier) + leads.
2. **Developers/contractors** → project mini-site (`nadlan_project`) + lead form + campaign.
3. **Private sellers/buyers** → promoted listing.
4. **Advertisers/brands** → banner + sponsored article (labeled ממומן).

**Phase 0 — NOW (pre-traffic). Sell only asset-based + performance-based:**
- **Pay-per-lead** (carry the risk ourselves): ₪90-150 new-apartment, ₪80-140 investment, ₪50-80 mortgage/lawyer. Value is self-evident — they pay per lead delivered.
- **Founding-member directory**: discount Pro/Premier as "founding" (e.g., free-to-₪149/mo for 6-12 months) in exchange for a testimonial. Honest because we're transparent it's launch pricing.
- **Developer/seller asset pages** priced as a *deliverable* (page built, X photos, 3D tour, lead form, live for N months) — NOT as traffic. Reprice the ₪10,990/19,990 developer SKUs as launch/performance until traffic exists.
- ❌ Do NOT sell ₪19,990 "exposure" campaigns or guaranteed-views sponsored content yet.

**Phase 1 — some traffic (3-6 mo): add** sponsored articles with **transparent live stats** (no guarantee yet), directory subscriptions at real price, listing promotion (₪49-199 highlight, 60-180 days, like WinWin).

**Phase 2 — established traffic: add** guaranteed-minimum-views sponsored content (TheMarker model, with make-good), banner CPM (₪4-20), premium developer campaigns, full rate card.

## D. Can we commit on traffic? How to report?
- **Today: NO traffic commitments** (we have neither traffic nor measurement). Commit only on what we control: the **asset** (page live for N months, spec'd), **position** (top of category for the period), and **deliverables** (article, 3D, lead form). For audience, sell **pay-per-lead** so we carry the risk.
- **Install measurement FIRST** (the #1 blocker): GA4 + Google Search Console + Bing Webmaster (Bing also powers ChatGPT Search). Then per-advertiser **monthly report**: impressions, page views, clicks, leads, time-on-page, search queries.
- **Once traffic is proven:** offer a **guaranteed minimum** (e.g., "≥X unique visitors in 30 days or we extend free / refund") — TheMarker's 1,500-uniques model — never before we reliably exceed it. Add **call-tracking numbers** (Rightmove model) for attribution.

## E. Legal must-dos (Israel)
1. **Sponsored-content disclosure is mandatory** — Consumer Protection Law §7(c): advertising disguised as an article is misleading even if true. Label every paid/benefit-driven piece clearly at the top: **"תוכן שיווקי" / "ממומן" / "בשיתוף"**, prominent and unambiguous (AG standard). A *benefit* (not just cash) triggers the duty. Fines ~₪29,490-53,070/violation (§22g) + class-action risk. **Build a reusable "ממומן" label block now.**
2. **Broker-license carve-out** — Brokers Law §2(c): publishing listings/info about interested parties needs **NO broker license**, as long as we (a) never call the site/staff "מתווך", (b) use no broker-implying title, (c) never personally match a specific buyer+seller for a transaction fee or take a % of a deal. **Stay a publisher/advertising platform**: sell listings, sponsored content, banners, and *advertising leads* priced as media — never transaction commissions. §9: brokers need a signed written order to collect fees — another reason to stay on the publishing side.

## F. End-to-end readiness checklist → "ready to take money"
1. [ ] Install GA4 + GTM + Search Console + Bing (measurement) — **blocker #1**
2. [ ] Build `/advertise/` (media kit: audience, products, deliverables, honest current stats) + fix `/pricing/`
3. [ ] Reusable **ממומן disclosure** label block + a sponsored-content policy on /editorial-policy/
4. [ ] Reprice developer/exposure SKUs to launch/performance until traffic exists
5. [ ] Seed the directory (recruit founding professionals; build 5-10 real project pages) so shelves aren't empty
6. [ ] Per-advertiser reporting template (monthly PDF/dashboard)
7. [ ] Terms of service for advertisers (deliverable spec, no traffic guarantee in Phase 0, make-good policy later)
8. [ ] Lead-product fulfillment flow (capture → dedupe → deliver → bill) on the existing lead endpoint
9. [ ] Verify checkout/invoice (Grow/Bit + Green Invoice/Morning) end-to-end with a test order

## G. Money goal — realistic phasing
- **Phase 0 revenue** = pay-per-lead + founding directory + asset-based developer/seller pages. Modest but HONEST; builds testimonials + case studies.
- **Phase 1** = + sponsored content (transparent stats) + paid listings + directory subscriptions.
- **Phase 2** = + guaranteed-views sponsored + banner CPM + premium developer campaigns + full rate card. This is where "lots of money" lives — but it is unlocked by traffic, which is unlocked by the content + ranking work already underway.

_Created 2026-05-31 by Claude Code. Sources: global portal research (Zillow/Rightmove/REA/Apartments.com/Redfin terms + media kits) and Israel research (Yad2/Madlan/Yad1/Komo, TheMarker media kit, leady.co.il lead list, Consumer Protection Law §7(c)+§22g, Brokers Law §2(c)+§9). Full source URLs in the session research transcripts._
